74. **Truncation repairs are disclosed to the model (forgecode takes review follow-up; extends ruling 67).** When JSON repair closed a CUT-OFF argument blob for a read-only tool (`read`, `grep`, `glob`, `repo_map`, `tool_search`, `ast_search`; the only tools whose truncated blobs are admitted), the admitted args may be incomplete and the `repaired: true` flag on the event and card is invisible to the model. `parseToolArguments` now also returns `truncated: true` (set only when the parser closed a cut-off tail, never for a plain syntax repair or type coercion), and `executeToolCall` in `loop.ts` appends one line, `[arguments were cut off and closed; re-issue the call if the values are incomplete]`, to the model-facing tool message text. The receipt and event digests are unchanged (the note is transcript text only, like the ruling 70 attempts note). No prompt line was added. Tests: `json-repair-loop.test.ts` (cut-off read carries the note; plain syntax repair and clean call do not).
75. **The pending-todos reminder fires only for a list this run modified (forgecode takes review follow-up; refines ruling 69).** Ruling 69 checked `openTodos` on every run, so stale open items left by an earlier run re-triggered the one-shot reminder (and an extra provider step) on every later, unrelated run of the session. `tools/todo.ts` now keeps a per-session mutation counter (`todoVersion`, bumped by a successful `add` or `done`; `list` does not bump it). `runTurn` snapshots it at run start and passes it to `pendingTodosReminder(sessionId, sinceVersion)`, which returns undefined while the counter is unchanged. A run that adds or completes any item re-arms the reminder for the whole list, so leftovers it created or half finished are still named. The reminder remains one-shot per run and the signature change is backward compatible (the second argument is optional). `docs/09` does not describe this behavior. Tests: `pending-todos.test.ts` (stale items, three runs on one session; version gate unit test).
76. **Structured compaction summary (forgecode take T-7).** `summariseElided` in `app/src/core/compaction.ts` now says what the elided turns DID, not only which tools ran: `- files touched (last op, oldest first): path (op)` (each path's LAST operation, in order of last touch; newest 20, older ones counted; long paths keep their tail), `- commands run` (shell commands and started `shell_session` commands, oldest first, newest 8, each clipped to 120 chars; `shell_session` stdin writes are not listed), `- searches` (`grep`/`glob`/`ast_search` pattern, path and glob; newest 6) and `- todo changes` (items added and ids done). A pure `classifyTool` maps the builtin names (`read`; `edit`/`ast_edit`; `write`; `apply_patch`, whose `*** Add/Update/Delete File` and `*** Move to` headers become write/edit/delete/moved per path; `undo`; search; shell; `todo`); any other tool's path arguments are listed with the tool name as the op, which replaces the old `paths seen` line. Every section is omitted when empty. The block is hard-capped at `MAX_SUMMARY_CHARS` (2500): optional lines are dropped whole and a cap note is written, while the heading, the counts line and the closing `view, not a deletion` line always survive. It stays a pure function of `(elided, total, limit)` derived only from tool-call arguments; tool results are not parsed, so a failed call is reported as attempted (there is no reliable error marker in result text). The pinned-constraints block is unchanged. Not taken from forge: cwd-prefix stripping (it needs the workspace path, so paths stay as the model wrote them) and the last reasoning block (ruling R30 forbids treating reasoning as evidence). Tests: `compaction.test.ts` (last op per file, command order and cap, todo changes, determinism, empty sections, size cap, classifier).
77. **Custom commands as files (forgecode take T-13).** `app/src/core/commands.ts` loads `*.md` prompt templates from `<workspace>/.hz/commands/` (and `~/.config/horizon/commands/`; the workspace wins). Optional front matter `description:`; the body is the template and `{{args}}` (or `$ARGUMENTS`) is replaced by the text after the name, otherwise the args are appended. `/name args` in the TUI, `hzcode -p "/name args"` and `hzcode run name args` expand the template and send it as an ordinary user message. A command is prompt text only and carries no authority: every action the model then takes still goes through the guard and gets a receipt. Builtin slash names always win (a file named `help.md` is ignored and listed in `/help`); a command shadows a skill of the same name; malformed or oversized files are skipped. `hzcode run x` falls back to normal chat when no command `x` exists. Tests: `tests/core/commands.test.ts`.
78. **Skill folders and foreign skill sources (forgecode take T-14).** `core/skills.ts` now also reads `skills/<name>/SKILL.md`; the other files in the folder (depth 3, at most 40) are listed by relative path in the active-skill block so the model reads them on demand, nothing is inlined. Flat `*.md` skills still load. Read-only extra sources: `~/.agents/skills`, `~/.claude/skills` and `<workspace>/.claude/skills`. Precedence, later wins: `~/.agents`, `~/.claude`, horizon global, workspace `.claude/skills`, horizon project (so project beats global; there are no built-in skills yet). Skills stay prompt text with no authority. Tests: `tests/core/skills-folders.test.ts` (the global tier is not unit-tested because `os.homedir()` ignores `$HOME` under Bun here).
79. **Symlinks are refused by the prompt-text readers; command lines are masked in the compaction summary (forgecode takes review follow-up).** `core/commands.ts` and `core/skills.ts` (flat files, `SKILL.md`, folder resources) use `lstat` and skip symlinks, so a repo link cannot put a sensitive file into the prompt around the guard's read policy. `maskSecrets` in `core/compaction.ts` masks Bearer tokens, `token=`/`key=`/`password=` style values and `sk-` style keys in the "commands run" lines of the summary.
80. **Conversation verbs (forgecode take T-12).** `/rename <title>`, `/retry` (drops the last reply via boundary-safe truncation and resends the last user message), `/stats` (message, tool call and local-estimate token counts), `/export [json|html]` (written to `<state>/exports/<session>.<ext>`; the HTML shows the receipt chain verdict and head hash from `receipt.verify`) and `/copy` (last answer to the clipboard via terminal OSC 52, no external tool). Logic in `core/convo.ts`; the five names are builtin, so a command file cannot shadow them. None of them changes what the guard sees.
81. **`hzcode config check` (forgecode take T-20).** `checkConfigFile` in `core/config.ts` reports what the loader silently ignores: unknown top-level keys, unknown keys inside `mcp`/`lsp`/`providers` entries, wrong-typed scalars, an unknown provider id, a bad `permissionMode`, and every entry the MCP, LSP and provider validators reject. Read-only, never echoes a value (only key paths and the validators' own messages), exits 1 when anything is reported and 0 when clean. **2026-11-10:** T-20 second half shipped — `CONFIG_KEYS`, `app/schema/hzcode.schema.json`, `tests/core/config-schema.test.ts`.

361. **R172 receipt-v2 cryptographic sidecar binding; v1 legacy compatibility (2026-11-14; PARTIAL, R172 OPEN).** Additive `schemaVersion=2` on all NEW sidecar-bearing receipts binds `effectId`, `postimageDigest`, `postimagePath` and `childEffectIds` into the receipt preimage, hash and chain anchor on both Rust and TypeScript. `kernel/src/receipt_schema.rs` validates version/field shape, `receipts.rs` issues v2 before hashing, and `eventlog.rs` validates/anchors versioned preimages; TS `receipt-schema.ts` mirrors it for `LocalKernel`, independent `receipt-chain-verify` and `chain-anchor-verify`. Frozen historical v1 digests are untouched, but v1 sidecars remain *unbound* and are not silently promoted. Trusted Rust↔TS mixed-chain/tamper tests `receipt-schema.test.ts` 4 PASS; Rust schema unit PASS; TypeScript typecheck PASS; `owner_` regressions run separately. **Still OPEN:** independently authenticated receipt issuer, full terminal/parent/child semantics, nested stream replay, all receipt issuance entrypoints, and universal effect observation.

359. **RC-18 canonical prompt admission, fail-closed before model dispatch (2026-11-14; PARTIAL).** `EventTypes.sessionPromptAdmitted` is the literal `hz.session.prompt.admitted` emitted after the SQLite admission FIFO returns `begin=true` and before the provider/tools are called. Payload has session/admission ids and SHA-256 + byte count of text, never the text. The event append is mandatory, not the historical best-effort log: a corrupt/missing audit chain stops the turn and latches its admission UNKNOWN rather than letting unrecorded work run. `rc18-admission.test.ts` verifies one hashed anchored real event and no provider dispatch on tampered anchor. **Not** atomic Bun/SQLite/kernel admission or independently human-attested authority; R170/P0-1 OPEN.

After 359 the verified static count is **24 shipped, 21/44 mapped, 10 equivalent, 4 literal, 23 unmapped**. These are catalogue/producer references, not a production emission or receipt replay certificate. `event-catalogue.test.ts` pins each count; all 44 must be actually instrumented/validated for full RC-18. Status OPEN.

The inspector names valid, hash-walk-only unanchored, invalid and missing child streams separately; it never claims child receipt replay or independent authority from a keyless chain. `receipt-child-session-stream.test.ts` adds valid parent/child chains, tampered child, tampered parent and missing stream cases (4/4 PASS; TypeScript check PASS). Full nested streaming, authoritative child receipts and all R172 done bars remain OPEN.
356. **P0-2 bounded archived historical direct-file postimages (2026-11-14; PARTIAL, NOT P0-2 closure).** Rust `effect_history.rs` independently double-reads new settled kernel-owned file postimages, pins their SHA-256 into hashed OwnerLog `postimages.archive` entries and publishes ≤64-MiB content blobs under `<state>/owner-postimages/<2hex>/<digest>.blob` with no-clobber hard-link, data+directory fsync and Unix 0700 shards. Failed archiving records `owner.unknown` before any receipt/settlement. Additive `effect.owner.postimage.verify.history` independently checks all archived versions, including overwritten ones; legacy bytes, absent-path facts and shell effects remain separately unverified. `effect.owner.verify` now rejects verified blob corruption. Rust unit/integration and TypeScript trusted-kernel regression tests cover supersession/tamper, with the code-verified remaining gaps in `audit/p0-eight-requirements-2026-11-14.md`. **Still OPEN:** all eight user acceptance requirements, historic legacy bytes and every non-file effect class, universal ownership and enforced network isolation.

The broader Bun suite and unrelated TUI approvals remain separate acceptance gates.
354. **Kernel AST projection rejects lossy source encodings (2026-11-14; TESTED, P0 still OPEN).** `astEditKernelProjection` now refuses NUL-bearing files and malformed UTF-8 before planning a single-site `fs.edit`, matching the existing multi-site `fs.write` restriction. `classifyAstEditKernelProjectionMiss` names `invalid_encoding` instead of deriving a misleading result from replacement characters. `effect-kernel-tool-projection.test.ts` checks malformed bytes and NUL for single- and multi-site projection and refusal classification; focused tests and TypeScript typecheck passed. Non-strict Bun fallback, unsupported multi-file effects, historical postimages and universal Rust ownership remain OPEN.

Stale/ambiguous/unrepresentable/multi-file plans refuse before any step; kernel result/receipt observation is retained. Targeted Rust/TypeScript kernel route tests passed (35/35 in two suites prior to ruling 354). Does not provide general `fs.batch`, cross-file atomicity, fuzzy patch parity or complete EffectHost authority.
352. **P0-1 trusted-parent permission and receipt-mint control channel (2026-11-14).** **A:** The product Bun `KernelClient` generates an ephemeral 256-bit credential in a JS hard-private field, delivers it over inherited fd 3 (private Unix socket/pipe, not argv/env), and passes a proof only with `guard.lease`, `guard.grant`, `guard.allowlist.amend` and caller-facing `receipt.issue` (not kernel-internal OwnerLog settlement). Rust `kernel/src/control_auth.rs` reads this descriptor before NDJSON service, validates strict lowercase 64-digit hex, validates open fd3 before owned-fd adoption (no missing-fd IO-safety abort), and compares proofs in fixed-length byte time; malformed/missing boot and raw forged permission/receipt RPCs refuse. A workspace-bound kernel without a private parent channel cannot mint permission. **B:** The client refuses permission/receipt minting through the local fallback after trusted transport loss or in untrusted `HZ_EFFECT_HOST_KERNEL=require`; an invalid/non-executable child registers its error handler before checking fd 3, eliminating the new EACCES crash. **C:** Bun test cases cover forged/raw lease, grant, allowlist RPC, valid session lease, malformed boot, trusted-loss privileged refusal and fallback regression; Rust control-auth unit tests and workspace-bound integration cover missing proofs. **Verified:** Rust integration **78 PASS**; Rust unit **118 PASS** (one slow filtered); focused Bun **50 PASS**, broad targeted **317 PASS** (39 files), typecheck PASS; invalid/missing private fd3 exits 2 without IO abort; product raw `receipt.issue` cannot forge, parent calls still work. Full Bun history: 352 initial **2,733 PASS / 57 FAIL / 3 SKIP** plus runner errors; after EACCES fix recorded rerun **2,747 PASS / 46 FAIL / 3 SKIP**, still NOT GREEN; latest additive receipt/fd checks verified focused, not by another complete full run. **NOT COMPLETE:** parent process proof is NOT independently authenticated human consent; parent runtime can call public mint APIs, the secret travels on NDJSON for privileged methods, standalone unbound kernels remain legacy-permissive, native Windows fd3 is unsupported, Bun/MCP/hooks/code-mode/SQLite remain non-atomic, shell netns/proxy/TLS unverified, historical postimages/patch parity/R172/RC-18 incomplete. All P0-1–4 and Wave 0 completion gates OPEN.
351. **P0-1 Guard-bound direct Rust admission, launch-time workspace binding and multi-resource lease evidence (2026-11-14).** **A:** `kernel/src/effect_owner.rs` now receives the live `Guard` from `kernel/src/protocol.rs` and checks each mutating direct `effect.execute` **inside the same POSIX OwnerLog lock**, after verifying chains and optional preimage and before writing `owner.prepare`: fs.write/edit/delete map to the existing `fs.write` Guard kind, rename checks **both** symlink-resolved endpoints as `fs.write`, and shell executes only with a Guard `allow` for its argv-derived `shell.exec` target. Policy `ask` and `deny` produce an immediate refusal with **zero OwnerLog prepare rows** and no filesystem effect; mixed-case kinds stay normalized. **B:** when `KernelClient` starts the real Rust binary, it overrides `HZ_KERNEL_WORKSPACE_ROOT` to the configured workspace; the Rust kernel captures that root at launch and refuses mutating raw RPCs whose claimed `sandboxRoot` is not the bound workspace. Standalone Rust daemons without a launch-time root remain unbound. **C:** the hashed OwnerLog `owner.prepare`/`owner.settled` now record `guardDecision=allow`, `guardLeaseId`, and complete `guardLeaseIds` for dual-endpoint renames; the existing receipt v1 preserves one hashed `leaseId` (last consumed) and `verify_settlement_receipt_links` independently checks it and all OwnerLog authorization IDs. Caller-supplied `leaseId` is ignored for kernel authorization. **D:** `tryKernelShellRoute` accepts explicit context only after the normal tool-loop Guard + human approval + pre-hook, and can mint a fresh exact-argv, session-scoped, one-use Rust lease before dispatch. A direct raw call without loop admission cannot skip the kernel's default ASK. **Evidence:** `kernel/tests/integration.rs` **78 PASS** including deny/ask/no-prepare, dual-lease rename, raw shell ask, root-widen refusal and cross-ledger binding; Rust unit **115 PASS** (one slow filtered); TypeScript typecheck PASS, Bun targeted **296 PASS** across 38 files. Full Bun **2,746 PASS / 43 FAIL / 3 SKIP**, NOT GREEN; global cargo fmt unchanged drift. **NOT P0 COMPLETE:** `guard.lease`/`guard.grant` raw RPCs still mint without an independently authenticated human principal, sessionId is caller-supplied, default Bun/hook/MCP/code-mode/child effects are not universally kernel owned, external filesystem/policy TOCTOU and cross-process SQLite admission persist, no production netns/proxy/TLS enforcement, full receipt/historical replay and atomic patch parity absent. All four P0s, R172, RC-18 and session/kernel universal consistency remain OPEN.
350. **P0-2 independent latest-settled file postimage verifier and partial R172 RC-7 owner terminal semantics (2026-11-14).** **A (Rust evidence):** `kernel/src/effect_owner.rs` now records additive `postimages` on `owner.settled` as hash-chained OwnerLog facts: `path/state/digest` for direct kernel-owned `fs.write` and `fs.edit`, absent-path evidence for `fs.delete`, and both source-absent and destination-content evidence for `fs.rename`; the file content digest comes from existing independent double-read and only records after a stable result. An unreadable/unstable absent path (not merely `exists() == false`) causes an UNKNOWN, never a settled claim. **B (standalone verifier):** `effect.owner.postimage.verify` / `KernelClient.ownerPostimageVerify()` independently re-read the latest settled postimage per path under the cross-process OwnerLog lock, after verifying owner and receipt chains; returns `ok/checked/legacyUnverifiable/unsupportedEffects/unresolved/mismatches` and an explicit `scope=latest_settled_file_postimage_only`. This catches an externally modified file with both hash chains still valid, accepts a superseding second kernel write and independently checks delete/rename source/destination. Historic overwritten images are NOT reconstructed, old settlements without evidence remain legacy-unverifiable, and shell effects count as unsupported (never count shell stdout hashes as network/FS proof). No ambient Bun mutation or production netns route is claimed verified. **C (R172 RC-7 reduction):** `owner.settled` records `terminal=succeeded` or `failed` for nonzero process exit (a failed shell exit can still yield an independently settled receipt); `owner.unknown` records `terminal=unknown`; a proven prepare-only crash `owner.released` is `interrupted`. This does NOT implement the full EffectReceiptV1, nested event streams, all entrypoints or RC-18 (canonical `hz.*` count unchanged). **Tests:** `kernel/tests/integration.rs` **74 PASS**, independent kernel processes, stale external overwrite, superseding latest write, rename/delete absence, shell unsupported, RC-7 nonzero exit and interrupted recovery; `app/tests/core/effect-owner-kernel.test.ts` cross-client tamper, method-catalogue consistency and untrusted fallback refusal. Final targeted Bun **294 PASS / 0 FAIL** (38 files); full Bun **2,743 PASS / 43 FAIL / 3 SKIP**, NOT GREEN; global rustfmt fails unchanged drift. **P0-1–4, full R170, full R172, RC-18, full patch parity and product network enforcement remain OPEN.**
349. **P0 R170 v2 safe pre-execution release, strict route refusal finality and pinned preimages (2026-11-14).** **A — Versioned Rust owner protocol:** `kernel/src/effect_owner.rs` writes additive `owner.prepare` with `protocolVersion:2`, followed by fsynced `owner.executing` BEFORE any mutating primitive. It retains `owner.settled` / `owner.unknown`, adds kernel-only `owner.released` and `effect.owner.recover({effectId})`. Under the SAME persistent POSIX `flock`, recovery reconstructs/verifies the owner/receipt chains and releases ONLY a v2 `prepared` intent with NO executing marker; effectId remains permanently reserved. Crashed, executing, UNKNOWN, settled, or legacy pre-v2 preparations CANNOT release via this proof. Debug-only `HZ_EFFECT_OWNER_FAULT_AFTER_EXECUTING=1` exits 87 after fsynced execution marker (ruling 348 already tests exit 86 after prepare); independent kernel-process tests prove clean release after 86, unsafe denial after 87, and replay ID refusal. `effect.owner.status` reports additive `recoverableIds`. **B — Authoritative transport / route fail-close:** `KernelClient.rpc` refuses local fallback for lost `effect.execute` transport (effect could have happened), and a formerly trusted kernel never silently resumes LocalKernel mutations after its loss. Strict `HZ_EFFECT_HOST_KERNEL=require` explicitly refuses untrusted LocalKernel mutators. `effect-kernel-mutation-route.ts` surfaces Rust `KernelRefusal` as a terminal failed `ToolResult` (not `undefined` / ambient Bun fallback); trusted `KernelClient.checkGuard` uses verified OwnerLog preflight to deny ambient mutators if UNKNOWN/tampered and refuses after trusted transport loss. This preflight is NOT atomic execution ownership for Bun tools. **C — No partial patch masquerade:** all `apply_patch` projections with more than one filesystem step refuse BEFORE any kernel write, including same-file multi-hunk / cross-file / rename chains; one-step projections still route. This deliberately reduces functionality until a Rust atomic `fs.batch` can preserve multi-step semantics. **D — Independent expected preimage:** additive `dispatch.spec.expectedPreimageDigest` (lowercase SHA-256, or null to assert new `fs.write`) is independently double-read and verified by Rust while holding OwnerLog before prepare and again immediately before direct fs write/edit/delete/rename. TS kernel-route write/edit/delete/rename sends the digest when a readable preimage exists; stale projections refuse without mutation or poison/UNKNOWN row. This is NOT a kernel-authorized workspace grant and does not eliminate external filesystem TOCTOU outside the owner lock. **Evidence:** `kernel/tests/integration.rs` 71 PASS incl. preimage mismatch/no prepare, v2 pre-execution recover, execution-start crash, cross-process unknown; `kernel/src/effect_owner.rs` legacy v1 recovery refusal; `app/tests/core/effect-owner-kernel.test.ts` and `effect-kernel-mutation-route.test.ts` 42 focused tests PASS with typecheck. **Residual / NOT COMPLETE:** Bun initially-untrusted `off/default` mutators, hooks/MCP/code mode/child processes, Rust guard-bound intent/approval enforcement, full atomic multi-file/AST/fs.edit fuzz parity, independent historic file/network postimage replay, cross-process Bun↔kernel admission, UNKNOWN resolution after execution marker, R172 event/nested terminal, RC-18 23/44, and product-routed netns/proxy/TLS deny tests. All P0-1–4 remain OPEN; shell-net must never be labeled `enforced` without product deny proof.
348. **P0 R170 kernel-owned atomic mutation OwnerLog precursor; raw kernel state/shell hardening (2026-11-13).** **A (Rust ownership):** new `kernel/src/effect_owner.rs` wraps every supported direct mutating `effect.execute` (`fs.write/edit/delete/rename`, `shell.exec`) in one permanent-inode POSIX `flock` held across `owner.prepare` (fsynced, hash-chained, reserved `owner:effects` stream) → kernel execute → independent file/stream observe → kernel `receipt.issue` → `owner.settled`. Unexpected execute/observe error durably appends `owner.unknown` (or leaves an unpaired `owner.prepare` after crash); all subsequent participating kernel mutations refuse `HZ_PRECONDITION_FAILED: OwnerLog UNKNOWN` until independently reconciled. Distinct kernel processes share the lock; mixed-case `FS.WRITE`/ `Fs.WrItE` dispatch is normalized identically to execution (a RED/green bypass regression), and duplicate `effectId` replay refuses without write. A development-only fault injector `HZ_EFFECT_OWNER_FAULT_AFTER_PREPARE=1` exits 86 *after fsynced prepare* and proves a restarted kernel blocks the dangling intent. `effect.owner.status` checks its owner chain; `effect.owner.verify` additionally verifies the complete receipt chain and matches every settled owner record against the corresponding kernel-issued receipt (id/seq/hash, resultDigest, prepare argsDigest/dispatchKind and the unhashed receipt effectId sidecar). This cross-ledger verification also runs before further owner mutation, refusing an invalid sidecar even when `receipt.verify` itself remains green. `protocol.rs` refuses caller `log.append` to reserved owner streams. **B (raw RPC protection):** symlink-aware file target overlap with the kernel state tree is refused for write/edit/delete and both rename endpoints before prepare; unconfined raw `shell.exec` refuses arbitrary binaries/interpreters/network clients before spawn and allows only a fixed set of output-only programs (`echo`, `true`, `false`, `pwd`). **C (additive TS contract):** `KernelRequestMap` exposes read-only owner status/verify and owner receipt sidecars; `KernelClient` refuses owner-status assertions on the untrusted LocalKernel fallback and disallows `log.append` to owner streams. Tests: `kernel/tests/integration.rs` 68 green including cross-process UNKNOWN, duplicate-ID and case bypass, debug-only crash-after-prepare, receipt-sidecar cross-check, stream-forgery, chain tamper, safe shell, state-dir alias/rename and explicit unconfined shell refusal; `app/tests/core/effect-owner-kernel.test.ts` trusted two-client postimage/receipt, concurrency and fallback refusal; `kernel/src/effect_owner.rs` unit malformed/orphan chain. The negative fs.write case in `hz-kernel --selftest` has an independent state dir so the valid UNKNOWN latch cannot be silently bypassed. **PARTIAL:** not a universal Rust EffectHost / guard-authorize proof: Bun ambient/default tools, direct hook/MCP/code-mode/process effects and other entrypoints do **not** share this atomic owner; no independent clean-release/replay recovery for an interrupted UNKNOWN, and cross-ledger receipt linkage + chain integrity are not historical file-postimage replay; no full R172 or RC-18 (internal `owner.*` events are not new canonical `hz.*` types); no default routed netns or shell-network `enforced`; `ops/usernetns-direct-tcp-denial-smoke.sh` proves TCP denial in a disposable isolated test namespace only (exit 0, errno 101), not product child networking. A local process able to replace kernel state files or lock inode remains a privileged adversary; raw shell whitelist is a conservative stopgap, not confinement. Full P0-1–4, R170, R172 and RC-18 **OPEN**.
347. **P0 / tasks 001–015 audit, kernel direct-edit anchored patch reduction, and lint spawn admission (2026-11-13).** **A (edit):** Under `HZ_EFFECT_HOST_EDIT_REPAIR=1`, Rust `effect_host_edit_repair.rs` and TypeScript `effect-host-edit-repair.ts` now reindent replacement text for uniquely matched per-line `trim` windows (3-way-style), preserve existing CRLF, and apply a last-resort *unique equal-length first/last nonblank anchored line-patch window* when no other rung matches. More than one anchor window returns ambiguous and leaves disk unchanged; the flag-off path remains exact-only. This is not a generalized 3-way merge/diff or full first-hit patch parity. Tests: `app/tests/core/effect-host-edit-repair.test.ts`, Rust repair tests and `app/tests/core/effect-execute-kernel.test.ts` trusted Rust disk/result-postimage-digest and ambiguity E2E. **B (admission):** `loop.ts` maps `lint` to `fs.read` and `present` to explicitly classified session-state (the tool already guards each inspected file). Previously an unmapped registry path blocked both despite their presence in the builtin registry. Selection happens over the complete JSONL population **before** `pendingBatchRows` so completed rows never shift a resumed slice. `batch-run.ts` wires `--filter text`, `--shuffle [seed]`, `--slice start:end` for dry-run and status, validates malformed ranges/missing filters, reports subset size, and distinguishes zero matches from completed batches. 192. **§5.5 remaining dialect mid-stream toolconv (2026-11-12).** `drainCompleteHarmonyToolCalls`, `drainCompleteKimiToolCalls`, `drainCompleteGlmToolCalls`, `drainCompleteDeepseekToolCalls`, `drainCompleteGeminiToolCalls`, `drainCompleteGemmaToolCalls`, `drainCompleteQwen3ToolCalls` (Hermes reuse), `drainCompleteAnthropicInvokeCalls` (MiniMax reuse) + updated `wrap-*.ts` emit `tool_call` when a closed block arrives mid-stream. Tests: `toolconv-*-stream.test.ts` for each dialect above. Residual: per-dialect tool-result wire tuning; pi-native dialect (no closed marker in spec). (Renumbered from a duplicate 187.)
82. **Plan-file convention and `execute-plan` skill (forgecode take T-19).** `docs/32-plan-files.md` documents `.hz/plans/<date>-<slug>-vN.md` with `- [ ]` tasks and a verification list. `core/skills.ts` gains `BUILTIN_SKILLS` with `execute-plan`, resolved by `getSkill` after the on-disk skills (so a user or workspace skill shadows it) and deliberately not listed by `listSkills`/`skillsPromptSection`, which keeps the system prompt unchanged. Plans are written in plan mode through the ordinary staged-write path; no special write grant exists.
83. **`hzcode commit [--preview] [--receipt] [context]` (forgecode take T-16).** The staged diff (`git diff --staged`, argv spawn, capped at 24000 chars) goes to one ordinary model turn with a fixed prompt; the cleaned reply is the message. `--preview` prints it and stops. Otherwise `git commit -m '<message>'` runs through `runtime.runGuardedShell`, the shell tool path, so it is a `shell.exec` guard decision (ask by default, approval prompt on a TTY, denied when nobody can answer) and a `tool.execute` receipt. `--receipt` (opt-in) appends `HZ-Receipt: <hash>` naming the receipt chain head as of the commit, which is before the commit's own receipt. `suggest` from T-16 is not built.
84. **Bracketed paste (TUI part 3, item 1).** On a TTY the chat enables `ESC[?2004h`; `app/src/tui/paste.ts` (a pure state machine over readline's `paste-start`/`paste-end` keypresses) gathers the lines of a paste into ONE input; the echoed rows are erased and the user block shows `[pasted N lines]` while the full text is sent. A multi-line paste during an approval question is an empty answer (a denial), never a yes. Non-TTY input is untouched. Tests: `tests/tui/paste.test.ts` and a PTY case in `tests/tui/pty.test.ts`.
85. **Single-key approval card (TUI part 3, item 2).** In rich TTY mode the approval question is a bordered card (`app/src/tui/approval-card.ts`): tool, args summary (4 lines then `+N lines`), a low/medium/high risk, the guardian line and the key legend `y yes  n no  a always this session  Esc deny`. One key answers without Enter; Enter, Esc and Ctrl-C deny. Type-ahead can never approve: the chat line is wiped before the card and keys in its first 250 ms are dropped. `ApprovalUi.askKey` is optional; without it (non-TTY, NO_COLOR, tests) the line prompt `[y]es / [n]o / [a]lways` is byte-identical. "always" still mints the target-scoped session grant of ruling 32. Tests: `tests/tui/approval-card.test.ts`, PTY case in `tests/tui/pty.test.ts`.
86. **Diff gutters (TUI part 3, item 3).** Rich mode renders `turn.diff` and tool-card/approval previews through `app/src/tui/diff.ts`: a `▎ path` header, hunks as `⋯ lines 40-52`, a right-aligned line-number gutter `  41 │- old` / `  41 │+ new` (green/red signs, `bgAdd`/`bgDel` row backgrounds at colour level 2+, signs only at level 1), unchanged runs over 6 lines folded to `⋯ N unchanged lines`, and a cap of 40 rows with a dim `… N more lines` footer. The plain `diffBlock` is untouched, so non-TTY output stays byte-identical. Tests: `tests/tui/diff.test.ts`.
87. **Thinking display (TUI part 3, item 4; contract C3, additive).** `ProviderEvent` gains `{type:"thinking",text}` and `RuntimeEvent` gains `{type:"assistant.thinking",text}`, both optional: OpenAI-compatible `reasoning_content`/`reasoning` deltas and Anthropic `thinking_delta` blocks are mapped to them; the loop forwards them for display only and never stores them in history or the transcript. Rich mode buffers the text and commits one dim collapsed line `◆ Thought for 4.2s · N lines · /think to expand` when the next real event arrives; `/think` prints the last block. Plain/non-TTY output ignores the event (byte-identical), a provider that never streams reasoning emits nothing and the UI keeps only its `Thinking…` label. The mock provider streams a two-line block when the request contains "think hard" (test aid). Tests: `tests/tui/thinking.test.ts`, PTY case in `tests/tui/pty.test.ts`.
88. **Subagent start/end events (TUI part 3, item 5; contract C2, additive).** `RuntimeEvent` gains `subagent.start {agentId,name,depth,task,profile?}` and `subagent.end {agentId,name,depth,status: completed|failed|cancelled,durationMs,tools?,reason?,profile?}`, emitted by `runNested` around the child run (harness-side facade events like `subagent.event`; the kernel event catalogue is unchanged). Rich mode binds them to the `agent` tool card (exact child time and `· N tools` on the completion line, `failed in 12s (budget_exhausted)`, `cancelled in 5s`) or, when no card exists, draws the running and completed cells itself. Plain mode ignores them; an older runtime that never emits them keeps the part-2 heuristic. Tests: `tests/core/subagent-boundary.test.ts`, `tests/tui/subagent-cells.test.ts`.
89. **Tab completion and ghost text (TUI part 3, item 6).** `app/src/tui/completion.ts` (pure) completes `/name` (built-ins plus `.hz/commands` files, list re-read every 2 s) and `@path` (last word; workspace-relative, directories first with a trailing `/`, `.git`/`node_modules`/`.hz`/`.hzcode` hidden, dotfiles only when the typed prefix starts with `.`, never outside the workspace). It is wired as the readline `completer`, so Tab completes the common prefix and lists several matches. In rich mode a dim ghost suffix of the first candidate is drawn after the cursor when the cursor is at the end of an idle line, erased before readline handles Enter and redrawn after each key. `think` and `theme` join `BUILTIN_COMMANDS` so a command file cannot shadow them. `@file` stays plain text sent to the model. Tests: `tests/tui/completion.test.ts` (6), PTY case in `tests/tui/pty.test.ts`.
90. **`/theme` (TUI part 3, item 7; D10 reduced ruling 250).** `/theme dark|light|auto|mono` (no argument shows the current choice) pins the palette through `style.ts` (`setThemeMode`, `activeTheme`): dark/light select the role table, auto keeps `detectTheme()` (HZ_THEME, COLORFGBG), mono returns no colour from `fgCode`/`bgCode` so bold and dim remain. It never turns colour on: the detection ladder (`NO_COLOR`, `TERM=dumb`, not a TTY => level 0) still decides whether colour is used, and with `NO_COLOR` set the command says so. **Persisted:** `persistThemeMode` merges `theme` into user config; `createRuntime` applies `config.theme` on startup (`HZ_THEME` still overrides env detection inside auto). Tests: `tests/tui/theme.test.ts` (6), PTY case in `tests/tui/pty.test.ts`. Residual: user theme files, OSC 11, high-contrast (D10).
91. **TUI part 3 review hardening (pre-publish review).** Two blocking defects fixed, one commit each. (a) Pasted text could answer the approval card: after the 250 ms arm window, a bracketed paste of `y` (or text starting with `y`/`n`/`a`) reached the single-key handler as ordinary keypresses; `createPasteCollector` gained `pasting()` and the card ignores every key between `paste-start` and `paste-end` plus the markers (`tests/tui/paste.test.ts`). (b) `@path` completion wrote raw file names to the terminal through Tab and ghost text; names containing C0, DEL, C1 or bidi/invisible characters are now never offered (`tests/tui/completion.test.ts`). Em dashes were removed from the new prose, headers and the `/theme` and `/think` notices. Verified: typecheck clean, 1792 pass / 8 skip (root chmod) / 0 fail. Known and deferred: readline may echo the `y` in the card (cosmetic), ghost width uses string length, C1 bytes are not stripped by `sanitizeText`, see docs/13 Part 3 known gaps.

5. **CLOSED 2026-11-07 (wave 9) — guard precedence ships the `docs/11-authority-safety.md` R1 order.** Was: `kernel/src/guard.rs` evaluated deny → lease → allow-rule → default ask, so a matching `allow` rule beat a matching `ask`; tracked here as a divergence, not silently corrected in either the docs or the code. Now: deny > lease > ask > allow in `kernel/src/guard.rs` and the TS fallback, with tests `guard_ask_rule_beats_allow_rule` and `guard_lease_still_beats_a_matching_ask_rule`; lease semantics unchanged. The stale `contracts.ts` `DEFAULT_POLICY` comment was fixed in the same wave (no residual).
6. **CLOSED 2026-11-07 (wave 11) — canonical state paths ship canonical-first, legacy in place (RC-17).** One resolver, `app/src/core/paths.ts`: an existing `<workspace>/.hzcode/` is honoured in place forever (shipped names kept; nothing under `.hz` created; legacy wins when both exist), else the canonical tree is used — kernel `<ws>/.hz/state/kernel`, session DB `<ws>/.hz/state/hzcode.db` (`docs/12` §4.1), `spills/`, `skills/`, `history` mirrored under `state/`; user config: existing `~/.hzcode/` in place, else `~/.config/horizon/`. All call sites routed; exported names/signatures unchanged. Evidence: `tests/core/state-paths.test.ts` (8); suite 490 pass / 0 fail (44 files); trusted-kernel fresh-workspace smoke wrote to `<ws>/.hz/state/kernel`; legacy smoke kept everything under `.hzcode/`. Residuals: legacy→canonical migration is explicit future work; the standalone kernel default (no `--state-dir`) still names `~/.hzcode/kernel`; per-call resolution can still fork under a mixed-version concurrent run (stated in the resolver header).
7. **Chain anchors close tail truncation, with one documented residual.** Both implementations now write `*.head` (seq + hash of the last record) after every append, verify against it, and refuse to extend a chain whose tail disagrees; deleting whole trailing records and rewritten/corrupt anchors report `brokenAt` (tests: `kernel/tests/integration.rs` anchor suite, `app/tests/core/{fallback,interop}.test.ts`). Residual: an actor who can rewrite the log can also delete or rewrite its anchor, and an absent anchor is accepted as a legacy chain — the anchor raises the cost of careless or partial tampering, it is not a keyed proof. Keying/anchor-of-record shipped in wave 9 (ruling 12 amendment): with `HZ_ANCHOR_KEY` configured a rewritten chain or anchor cannot authenticate — a wrong key reports `brokenAt`, legacy anchors under a key are refused (`HZ_PRECONDITION_FAILED`) unless `HZ_ACCEPT_LEGACY_ANCHORS=1`, byte-for-byte in both implementations. Residual, recorded: keyless chains (and anchor deletion with no key configured) stay legacy-accepted — keying is opt-in.
8. **Cross-document contradictions resolved (RC-16).** The pass-5 audit's eight live corpus conflicts are closed by `_conventions.md` RC-16, with the owner documents amended in that same docs-only change: leases are per-effect under batch admission; wire errors are `HZ_*` and `LOG_*`/`SESSION_*`/dotted codes are internal; reason codes are the closed lowercase set; the session-log chain is sha256; `strict` (`synchronous=FULL`) is the durability default; `docs/12` §4.1 owns the layout with two-level CAS shards and workspace kernel state; `DurableEventV1` is the wire envelope with `evt_`/`apr_`/`bat_` registered and `approvalId` the one field name; `docs/30` §4 is the execution queue. No implementation change; the pass-5 note remains the record of what was proposed and why.
9. **CI runner landed (pass-5 C.3, ruling 17).** `bash ops/gates.sh` → 6 gates green (~13 s at wave time); the 18 design gates in `docs/24` §3.5 are all `declared-gap` with per-row reasons in `ops/gate-catalogue.md`; the workflow ships as the installable payload `ops/ci/github-actions.yml` (`ops/install-ci.sh`): the automation identity lacks GitHub's `workflow` scope (see ruling 17). **(Postscript 2026-11-10: the runner now has EIGHT gates: `ts-typecheck` was added by ruling 39 and `startup-latency` by ruling 72. Run as root on the `docs/handoff` tree all eight pass, because the permission tests that cannot bind for uid 0 now skip themselves by a behavioural probe (rulings 39 and 51) instead of failing. The earlier statement that the runner stops at `ts-suite` on 11 root-only failures is obsolete.)**
10. **Kernel state protection (wave 10, closes triage finding 6; shell residual mitigated 2026-11-12).** A hard-coded kernel rule denies any `fs.write` resolving inside the kernel state directory before policy and leases (reason `kernel-state-protected`; symlink/`..`-aware), and both TS transports refuse earlier with `HZ_PRECONDITION_FAILED` — the model can no longer grant itself permissions by editing `policy.json` through the write/edit tools; hot reload remains for human edits. **2026-11-12:** `shell.exec` whose command string names the kernel state dir is also structurally denied (TS `protectedPathRefusal` + Rust `shell_references_kernel_state`); wrapped spawns set `HZ_SANDBOX_FS_RO_PATHS` so Landlock carves the state dir RO under workspace-write. Residual: obfuscated multi-step shell without marker strings; unwrapped local fallback; EffectHost still open.
11. **OS-level sandbox for Code Mode and shell (NF-016) — PARTIALLY ADDRESSED (admission-time allowlist + egress decider + SandboxMode ladder landed; Linux `hz-sandbox-exec` confinement TESTED in rulings 140–150; MITM proxy + macOS/Windows still open).** FEAT-002 (ruling 42) landed the declarative command allowlist (`kernel/src/execpolicy.rs`) consulted for `shell.exec` BEFORE the policy rules — an allowlist deny wins (docs/11 R1), an allow admits, absence leaves `shell.exec`=ask byte-identical, a malformed allowlist fails closed, with an order-independent `fingerprint()` over the LAW hash and a file-locked runtime `guard.allowlist.amend`. FEAT-003 (ruling 43) added the companion per-origin egress decider (`kernel/src/egress.rs`) consulted for `net.fetch` BEFORE the policy rules — default-deny per origin (docs/11 N1/N2), observe mode, a two-level hold budget (16/8, 600s) and content-free `BlockedRequest` records, with absence of `egress.json` leaving `net.fetch`=ask byte-identical. FEAT-004 (ruling 44) added the fail-closed `SandboxMode` ladder (`kernel/src/sandbox.rs`), reachable over the additive `sandbox.report` method: the per-call `read-only`/`workspace-write`/`danger-full-access` modes with the strictly-`WIDER_MODES` table, byte-for-byte dsh escalation/denial markers, `validate_escalation_args` + one-shot `approve_escalation`, and a per-call enforcement-completeness (`full`/`partial`) guarantee vector with typed residuals. The OS enforcement backend is CAPABILITY-DETECTED at runtime and FAILS CLOSED: the enforcement SEAM now exists (the kernel reports an honest posture) but on every kernel without an active Landlock LSM — both the gVisor deployment class AND this build host (Linux 6.1.189-amzn2023: the `landlock_create_ruleset` symbol is present but `/sys/kernel/security/lsm` is absent, and `bwrap`/bubblewrap 0.12.0 being present does NOT by itself provide kernel-enforced FS confinement) — an FS-confined mode yields `SANDBOX_UNAVAILABLE` (docs/11 S1/S4), NEVER a silent `enforced` and NEVER silently unconfined; seccomp/rlimits/netns/userns are DETECTED and reported as `available`, never as `enforced`, because the kernel applies none of them (ruling 44 correction, ruling 51); a Landlock-capable host is reported the same fail-closed way; `danger-full-access` reports no enforced dimension (S4). All three are admission-time policy + a fail-closed report, NOT yet OS confinement; the kernel-executed effect host, the bubblewrap+Landlock/seccomp/netns backend that would make a mode `enforced`, the one-shot-widen runtime seam, and the routed MITM egress proxy (N10) remain the class fix. Still open: Code Mode runs with the user's ambient authority (bridge-policed only); `setsid` descendants escape group kills; hardlinks outside `policy.json` rely on chain detection; non-Linux fallbacks are documented as weaker; the allowlist's argv splitter is a reduced-form whitespace tokenizer (wrapper unwrapping and a tree-sitter per-segment split deferred — ruling 42). Environment facts measured 2026-11-08 (spike `docs/audit/landlock-spike-2026-11-08.md`, probe `ops/landlock-probe.sh`): no `bwrap`; Landlock is NOT available on this deployment kernel (`4.19.0-gvisor` — raw syscall ENOSYS, no LSM list); unprivileged user namespaces are NOT blocked (`unshare -U/-Ur/-Urm/-Uprf` succeed; bare `unshare -p` EPERMs, no CAP_SYS_ADMIN). Direction per ruling 38: design the seccomp + `no_new_privs` + rlimits + netns reduced form first; the outer sandbox is the confinement of record on gVisor-class deployments; Landlock stays probe-gated. Evidence for the allowlist (TESTED): `cd kernel && CARGO_HOME=/tmp/cargo-home cargo test --locked` (19 unit + 1 known root artifact, 40 integration); `./target/release/hz-kernel --selftest --state-dir $(mktemp -d)` → 21 checks; `cd app && bun run typecheck` → 0, `bun test` → 881/11 (known root set). Full residual list: `docs/audit/reaudit-2026-11-08.md` §6.
